**[00:05:23.230] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000039)

**[00:05:39.410] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000042)  
with release of mortgage, these are our COCROMs, covering 901,246 hectares.

of land and freeing hundreds of thousands

**[00:05:52.910] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000044)

**[00:21:59.370] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000387)

**[00:22:11.730] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000392)  
The annual figures also show

**[00:22:13.890] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000393)  
a sustained upward trajectory.

**[00:22:16.930] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000394)  
From 21,876 titles in 2022,

**[00:22:21.990] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000395)

102,536 in 2024,

**[00:22:32.570] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000398)

**[00:22:35.230] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000400)  
This sustained increase demonstrates the Department's determination to accelerate land distribution and provide beneficiaries with stronger tenure security.

**[00:22:51.370] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000401)

**[00:23:35.230] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000405)  
And approximately 57.76 billion in agrarian debt.

**[00:23:42.610] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000406)  
As we move toward completion, the Department remains focused on reaching every qualified beneficiary covered by the law.

**[00:23:51.150] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000407)  
This is the concrete expression of the President's directives that we complete the debt condonation process this year.

**[00:24:06.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000408)  
For fiscal year 2020,

**[00:24:09.360] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000409)  
With regards to agrarian justice delivery, the second pillar is agrarian justice delivery.

**[00:24:17.020] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000410)  
Agrarian reform cannot be complete if farmers have land but cannot obtain timely and accessible justice when disputes arise.

**[00:24:29.740] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000411)  
The Department therefore continues to strengthen its legal mechanisms to ensure that the agrarian disputes are resolved fairly,

**[00:24:38.780] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000412)  
efficiently, and effectively.

**[00:24:39.360] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000413)  
Our objective is clear to reduce case backlogs, improve the efficiency of legal processes, and bring justice closer to our farmers and other agrarian stakeholders.

**[00:24:59.220] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000414)  
This slide presents the performance of the agrarian implementation or ALI cases.

In 2022,

**[00:25:10.760] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000416)  
out of 7,644 cases,

**[00:25:27.340] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000420)

**[00:25:35.040] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000421)  
We discovered that there were many other cases that we,

**[00:25:40.760] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000422)

**[00:25:43.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000423)

representing 99.42%.

From January to June

9,874

or

**[00:29:07.660] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000507)  
These accomplishments demonstrates

**[00:29:10.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000508)  
the Department's continuing commitment

**[00:29:12.220] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000509)  
to strengthen the legal,

**[00:29:14.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000510)  
ethical foundations of agrarian reform

**[00:29:16.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000511)  
and ensure that disputes involving

**[00:29:18.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000512)  
farmers, beneficiaries,

**[00:29:20.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000513)  
tenancy, and leasehold

**[00:29:22.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000514)  
are addressed through appropriate

**[00:29:24.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000515)  
legal mechanisms.

**[00:29:28.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000516)  
The third pillar is

**[00:29:30.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000517)  
agrarian reform, beneficiaries,

**[00:29:32.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000518)  
development, and sustainability.

**[00:29:34.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000519)  
The third pillar is

**[00:29:36.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000520)  
agrarian reform

**[00:29:38.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000521)  
does not end

**[00:29:40.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000522)  
with the award of land.

**[00:29:42.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000523)  
Our responsibility is to help

**[00:29:44.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000524)  
ensure that our beneficiaries are

**[00:29:46.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000525)  
equipped with the resources, knowledge,

**[00:29:48.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000526)  
infrastructure, technology,

**[00:29:50.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000527)  
and market opportunities

**[00:29:52.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000528)  
necessary to make

**[00:29:54.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000529)  
their land productive and

**[00:29:56.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000530)  
their organizations sustainable.

**[00:29:58.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000531)  
Under this pillar,

**[00:29:59.720] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000532)  
DAR continues to provide support that enables our farmers not only to own land, but to make a viable and sustainable livelihood from it.

**[00:30:11.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000533)  
One of the important forms of support is irrigation.

**[00:30:14.700] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000534)  
From July 2022 to July 2026, DAR has facilitated 285 irrigation projects, of which 233 have been completed and 52 are ongoing.

**[00:30:28.980] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000535)  
These projects amount to P5.02 billion covering 34,711.74 hectares and benefiting 40,775 farmer beneficiaries.

**[00:30:46.480] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000536)  
Importantly, the majority of these irrigation facilities are solar-powered, providing a more sustainable and cost-efficient source of water.

**[00:30:58.980] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000537)  
Beyond increasing productivity, these also help reduce the time and effort required for water access, particularly for women involved in farming and household activities.

**[00:31:14.900] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000538)  
Through reliable and sustainable water infrastructure, we help farmers improve production, reduce vulnerability to weather conditions, and maximize the productive potential.

**[00:31:28.980] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000539)  
We have also continued to improve physical connectivity through farm-to-market roads.

**[00:31:36.780] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000540)  
From July 2022 to July 2026, DAR has completed 537 farm-to-market roads nationwide, covering a total distance of 435.807 kilometers and benefiting 155,123 agrarian reform.

**[00:31:58.980] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000541)  
There are also 89 ongoing projects covering approximately 108.351 kilometers and benefiting 26,953 ARBs.

**[00:32:14.620] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000542)  
These roads help address one of the persistent challenges faced by farmers, bringing their produce from the farm to the market.

**[00:32:25.640] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000543)  
Better connectivity means reduced transport costs.

**[00:32:28.980] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000544)  
Transportation difficulties improve access to markets and greater opportunities for our beneficiaries to increase their income.

**[00:32:38.780] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000545)  
Modern agriculture requires modern tools.

**[00:32:48.240] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000546)  
As part of our support services, DAR has distributed 13,009 farm machineries and equipment, including support facilities, benefiting 3,207 agrarian reform beneficiaries.

**[00:33:03.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000547)  
These interventions are intended to improve productivity, reduce production costs, and strengthen the capacity of ARB organizations to undertake agricultural activities at a more efficient and competitive scale.

**[00:33:20.740] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000548)  
Our objective is to ensure that the land awarded to our beneficiaries is supported by the appropriate resources to make it productive and sustainable.

**[00:33:33.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000549)  
Capacity building remains as one of the most important investments we make for our agrarian reform beneficiaries.

**[00:33:43.440] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000550)  
From July 2022 to July 2026, DAR recorded 1,605,635 training participants in terms of service count.

**[00:33:57.480] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000551)  
This training goes beyond seminars.

**[00:33:59.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000552)  
They prepare our farmers and their organizations.

**[00:34:03.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000553)  
To succeed and endure.

**[00:34:04.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000554)  
We have learned that many agriculture-based cooperatives do not fail because of a lack of dedication or hard work.

**[00:34:15.180] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000555)  
They fail because they lack three critical foundations, financial, technical, and managerial skills.

**[00:34:23.920] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000556)  
We are equipping our beneficiaries not only to cultivate the land, but also to manage their finances responsibly,

**[00:34:31.620] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000557)  
adapt modernly,

**[00:34:33.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000558)  
implement technologies,

**[00:34:34.740] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000559)  
lead their cooperatives effectively,

**[00:34:37.220] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000560)  
and build sustainable enterprises.

**[00:34:40.260] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000561)  
Enthusiasm may start a cooperative,

**[00:34:43.200] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000562)  
but knowledge and good management keep it alive.

**[00:34:47.520] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000563)  
This is the kind of empowerment we want to leave behind,

**[00:34:51.440] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000564)  
not just productive farms,

**[00:34:53.840] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000565)  
but capable farmers who can sustain their progress for generations.

**[00:35:00.940] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000569)  
Access to capital is equally important in enabling our farmers to become economically independent.

**[00:35:07.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000570)  
From July 2022 to July 2026,

**[00:35:11.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000571)  
DAR has facilitated access to credit and microfinance services

**[00:35:15.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000572)  
for 674,282 agrarian reform beneficiaries, service count beneficiaries.

**[00:35:25.720] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000573)  
Access to affordable financing provides farmers with opportunities to invest in production,

**[00:35:31.940] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000574)  
acquire necessary inputs,

**[00:35:34.460] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000575)  
improve their enterprises,

**[00:35:36.420] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000576)  
and expand their economic activities.

**[00:35:39.040] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000577)  
Ultimately, our goal is to help build financially capable

**[00:35:43.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000578)  
and self-reliant farmers who can contribute more significantly

**[00:35:48.120] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000579)  
to the development of their communities and the nation.

**[00:35:53.420] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000580)  
Marketing assistance is another important component

**[00:35:56.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000581)  
of our support to agrarian reform beneficiaries organization.

**[00:36:01.480] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000582)  
From July 2022 to July 2026,

**[00:36:04.460] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000583)  
DAR has linked 4,934 ARB organizations to market opportunities,

**[00:36:14.720] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000584)  
benefiting 1,041,308 ARBs in terms of service count

**[00:36:21.500] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000585)  
and generating 28.98 billion in total sales.

**[00:36:27.040] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000586)  
These gains are supported through various enterprise development and market linkage,

**[00:36:34.460] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000587)  
initiatives that help ARBOS strengthen their enterprises,

**[00:36:38.420] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000588)  
develop their products,

**[00:36:39.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000589)  
and gain more reliable access to markets.

**[00:36:43.380] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000590)  
We also partner with institutional buyers from both the public and private sectors,

**[00:36:49.220] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000591)  
creating more direct and sustainable market opportunities for our ARBOS.

**[00:36:55.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000592)  
By creating these more direct market linkages,

**[00:36:59.540] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000593)  
we help reduce the exposure of our farmers to predatory pricing,

**[00:37:04.240] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000594)  
and provide them with better opportunities to negotiate

**[00:37:07.460] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000595)  
and sell their products at fairer prices.

**[00:37:12.160] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000596)  
Ultimately, our goal is to ensure that the productivity gains of our beneficiaries

**[00:37:17.300] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000597)  
are translated into actual income,

**[00:37:20.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000598)  
stronger enterprises, and more sustainable livelihoods.

**[00:37:25.960] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000599)  
Another important initiative is the Partnership Against Hunger and Poverty, or PAHAP, project.

**[00:37:32.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000600)  
The program connects agrarians,

**[00:37:34.240] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000601)  
agrarian reform beneficiary organizations directly with government and private institutions

**[00:37:39.960] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000602)  
as suppliers of nutritious agricultural products,

**[00:37:43.900] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000603)  
including rice, vegetables, and other commodities.

**[00:37:51.500] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000604)  
This creates a direct link between farmers and institutional markets,

**[00:37:56.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000605)  
bypassing middlemen who engage in predatory pricing,

**[00:38:00.060] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000606)  
and ensuring farmers receive a more patriotic pricing for their produce.

**[00:38:06.960] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000607)  
From July 2022 to July 2026,

**[00:38:12.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000608)  
the program has generated approximately 9.972 billion in total gross sales.

**[00:38:20.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000609)  
It has linked 448 ARBOS to institutional markets

**[00:38:25.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000610)  
and served 13,440 ARBs with nationwide coverage.

**[00:38:31.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000611)  
The project is funded through the General Appropriations Act,

**[00:38:35.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000612)  
with a total project fund of approximately 208 million.

**[00:38:41.360] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000613)  
This demonstrates that government support can go beyond production.

**[00:38:46.840] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000614)  
By connecting farmers directly to markets,

**[00:38:49.640] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000615)  
we create opportunities for increased income,

**[00:38:52.920] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000616)  
stronger organizations, and more sustainable agricultural enterprises.

**[00:38:59.920] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000617)  
We now proceed to the department's budget performance and proposed allocation

**[00:39:05.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000618)  
for fiscal year 2027.

**[00:39:09.360] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000619)  
We subscribe to the principle that government spending,

**[00:39:13.720] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000620)  
when properly and efficiently implemented,

**[00:39:17.360] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000621)  
serves as a means of pump-priming the economy,

**[00:39:22.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000622)  
particularly when public resources translate into quality and meaningful investments.

**[00:39:30.120] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000623)  
In DAR, our programs and projects are therefore designed not only to deliver services,

**[00:39:35.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000624)  
to our agrarian reform beneficiaries,

**[00:39:38.800] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000625)  
but also to generate productive economic activity in the countryside.

**[00:39:44.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000626)  
Through our budget, we seek to ensure that every peso entrusted to the department

**[00:39:50.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000627)  
is spent efficiently, responsibly, and with the highest level of accountability.

**[00:39:57.480] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000628)  
Let us now look at our Budget Utilization Rate, or BUR.

**[00:40:03.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000629)  
In fiscal year 2023,

**[00:40:05.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000630)  
against an allotment of 17.49 billion,

**[00:40:10.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000631)  
DAR posted an obligation rate of 74% and a disbursement rate of 82%.

**[00:40:18.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000632)  
In fiscal year 2024,

**[00:40:21.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000633)  
against an allocation of 17.385 billion,

**[00:40:26.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000634)  
DAR posted an obligation rate of 88.67%

**[00:40:32.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000635)  
and a disbursement rate of 75%.

**[00:40:34.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000636)  
In fiscal year 2025,

**[00:40:39.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000637)  
with an allotment of 20.55 billion,

**[00:40:44.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000638)  
our obligation rate improved to 91.49%,

**[00:40:49.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000639)  
while our disbursement rate increased to 74.65%.

**[00:40:56.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000640)  
For fiscal year 2026, as of June,

**[00:40:59.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000641)  
our total allotment stood at 18.379 billion.

**[00:41:05.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000644)  
At mid-year, we recorded an obligation rate of 47%

**[00:41:10.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000645)  
and a disbursement rate of 70%.

**[00:41:14.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000646)  
This reflects that activities and services benefiting our agrarian reform beneficiaries

**[00:41:21.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000647)  
have been timely delivered.

**[00:41:23.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000648)  
These figures tell us two things.

**[00:41:28.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000649)  
First, the department has demonstrated that it can absorb and use the resources of the government.

**[00:41:36.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000652)  
Second, there remains room to further strengthen the pace of implementation,

**[00:41:42.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000653)  
particularly in translating allotments into obligations and obligations into actual disbursements.

**[00:41:51.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000654)  
As of mid-year, our obligation rate stands at 47%,

**[00:41:56.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000655)  
while our disbursement rate is already at 70%.

**[00:42:01.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000659)  
While we recognize the need to accelerate our disbursement rate,

**[00:42:07.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000660)  
it is positive indication that our existing obligations

**[00:42:11.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000661)  
are being translated into actual payments and program delivery.

**[00:42:16.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000662)  
For the remaining months of fiscal year 2026,

**[00:42:20.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000663)  
our task is clear—

**[00:42:23.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000664)  
accelerate implementation without sacrificing accountability and compliance.

**[00:42:30.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000665)  
is not spending for the sake of spending.

**[00:42:33.420] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000666)  
Our goal is responsible and judicious utilization

**[00:42:38.860] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000667)  
where every obligation represents a real commitment

**[00:42:43.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000668)  
and every disbursement represents an actual accomplishment

**[00:42:47.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000669)  
delivered to our beneficiaries.

**[00:42:51.860] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000670)  
Against this performance backdrop,

**[00:42:54.860] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000671)  
we now move to our proposed budget for fiscal year 2027.

**[00:42:59.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000672)  
The Department of Agrarian Reform has a total fiscal year 2027

**[00:43:06.020] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000673)  
national expenditure program of approximately 16.935 billion.

**[00:43:13.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000674)  
This represents the resources that will support

**[00:43:20.620] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000675)  
the Department's continuing implementation of agrarian reform

**[00:43:25.200] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000676)  
from securing land tenure and delivering agrarian justice

**[00:43:29.800] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000677)  
to providing support services to the people.

**[00:43:31.420] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000678)  
and implementing projects.

**[00:43:34.900] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000679)  
But this amount must again be viewed in terms of what we intend to accomplish with it.

**[00:43:42.100] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000680)  
What we in DAR continuously ask is,

**[00:43:46.320] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000681)  
how do we ensure that this 16.935 billion

**[00:43:50.160] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000682)  
produces the greatest possible value for our agrarian reform beneficiaries?

**[00:43:56.900] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000683)  
That requires sound planning,

**[00:44:01.420] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000684)  
disciplined financial management,

**[00:44:03.420] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000685)  
timely procurement,

**[00:44:05.420] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000686)  
reliable information systems

**[00:44:07.420] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000687)  
and strong accountability at every level of the Department,

**[00:44:12.220] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000688)  
and of course the support of our distinguished lawmakers.

**[00:44:16.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000689)  
Thank you in advance for your support.

**[00:44:21.380] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000690)  
Let us now break down the 16.935 billion fiscal year 2027.

**[00:44:34.350] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000691)  
Next.

**[00:44:36.830] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000692)  
According to expense class, approximately 7.066 billion or 41.72% is allocated for personal services.

**[00:44:50.310] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000693)  
Another 8.61 billion or 50.90% is allocated for maintenance and other operating expenses.

**[00:45:00.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000694)  
While 1.249 billion or 7.37% is allocated for capital outlays.

**[00:45:08.960] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000695)  
So, makikita po natin dito, mas maliit po ang kapital, mas malaki ang kita kung iahambing natin sa isang negosyo.

**[00:45:18.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000696)  
Hindi po shortchains ang ating government dito.

**[00:45:22.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000697)  
The largest share goes to MOOE which supports the actual implementation and day-to-day delivery of the department's programs and activities.

**[00:45:33.900] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000698)  
Personal services, meanwhile, provides the human resources necessary to carry out our mandate from the central office down to our regional, provincial, and municipal offices.

**[00:45:46.820] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000699)  
Capital outlays support necessary investments that strengthen the department's operations.

**[00:45:52.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000700)  
What is important is that these components work together.

**[00:46:00.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000701)  
We need people, we need operational resources, and we need the systems, equipment, and facilities that allow our people to work effectively.

**[00:46:10.020] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000702)  
Our responsibility is to ensure that this mix of resources remains aligned with the department's priorities and delivers the maximum benefit to our state.

**[00:46:22.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000703)  
As we look toward fiscal year 2027, our direction remains clear.

**[00:46:32.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000704)  
Sustain the gains we have achieved, complete our remaining debt condonation obligations, continue land distribution and strengthen tenure security, expand access to agrarian justice, and ensure that our beneficiaries have the means to make their land productive and sustainable.

**[00:46:52.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000705)  
For land tenure security, DAR will sustain the upward trajectory of distribution of split e-titles and remaining land titles, including CLOAS, and continue the implementation of Executive Order No. 75 Series of 2019.

**[00:47:12.260] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000706)  
We will also complete the distribution of the remaining certificates of condonation with the release of mortgage in the Bangsa-Moro Autonomous Region.

**[00:47:23.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000707)  
At the same time, we will strengthen engagement with stakeholders to accelerate the resolution of issues affecting the documentation of remaining land holdings.

**[00:47:36.800] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000708)  
Finally, the department will establish a more accurate and reliable inventory and data system through comprehensive digitalization of land distribution and tenure security records.

**[00:47:48.920] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000709)  
These measures are essential to ensuring that land distribution

**[00:47:52.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000710)  
is not only accelerated but properly documented, transparent, and sustainable.

**[00:48:01.440] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000711)  
For agrarian justice delivery, the Department will continue to streamline legal processes

**[00:48:07.440] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000712)  
and reduce delays in case resolution.

**[00:48:11.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000713)  
The implementation of the Electronic Certificate of No Pending Appeal or e-CONA system

**[00:48:18.520] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000714)  
will help address delays in the issuance of Certificates of No Pending Appeal

**[00:48:24.620] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000715)  
and facilitate the resolution and termination of cases.

**[00:48:29.920] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000716)  
DAR will also strengthen the training and institutionalization of alternative dispute resolution methods

**[00:48:37.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000717)  
among its legal personnel with the objective of achieving mutually acceptable outcomes among parties.

**[00:48:44.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000718)  
Consistent with DAR Memorandum 198,

**[00:48:47.740] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000719)  
our field implementors will be encouraged to maximize the use of agrarian reform justice on wills or ARJAL,

**[00:48:59.260] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000720)  
bringing free, adequate, and effective legal assistance closer to our farmer beneficiaries,

**[00:49:07.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000721)  
agricultural lessees, landowners, and other stakeholders.

**[00:49:11.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000722)  
This is particularly important for farmers who may otherwise have to leave their areas

**[00:49:17.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000723)  
simply to access legal assistance.

**[00:49:21.900] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000724)  
The Department will also strengthen its legal workforce to help draft and fast-track the resolution of agrarian law implementation cases,

**[00:49:32.020] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000725)  
consistent with our zero-caseload policy.

**[00:49:36.320] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000726)  
Our objective is straightforward, timely, accessible, and fair justice for our agrarian reform stakeholders.

**[00:49:46.540] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000727)  
Ladies and gentlemen, we subscribe to the dictum that justice delayed is justice denied.

**[00:49:57.160] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000728)  
That's why we are full speed ahead of the resolution of the pending cases.

**[00:50:11.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000729)  
Finally, under agrarian reform beneficiaries development and sustainability,

**[00:50:16.540] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000730)  
the Department will continue transforming agrarian reform beneficiary organizations into self-sustaining cooperatives.

**[00:50:26.780] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000731)  
This will be supported through several key interventions.

**[00:50:30.700] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000732)  
First, we will strengthen access to capital so that organizations have the financial capacity to expand their enterprises.

**[00:50:39.960] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000733)  
Second, we will promote secure market integration through public-private partnerships.

**[00:50:46.540] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000734)  
Third, we will encourage technological adoption to improve productivity and competitiveness.

**[00:51:03.060] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000735)  
And fourth, we will strengthen institutional capacity building,

**[00:51:08.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000736)  
ensuring that ARB organizations are equipped with the leadership, management, and organizational capabilities

**[00:51:17.060] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000737)  
necessary to sustain their operations.

**[00:51:20.980] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000738)  
The objective is to move our beneficiaries beyond dependence on government assistance

**[00:51:27.440] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000739)  
toward greater self-reliance, productivity, and sustainability.

**[00:51:33.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000740)  
Honorable members of Congress, distinguished guests, ladies and gentlemen,

**[00:51:39.340] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000741)  
the fiscal year 2027 proposed budget of the Department of Agrarian Reform

**[00:51:47.060] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000743)  
It is ultimately about more than figures and appropriations.

**[00:51:51.340] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000744)  
It is about fulfilling our commitments, securing the land and rights of our farmers,

**[00:52:00.060] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000745)  
and completing the historic debt condonation program mandated by Republic Act 11953

**[00:52:07.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000746)  
and directed by President Ferdinand R. Marcos, Jr.

**[00:52:12.520] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000747)  
With the resources entrusted to us,

**[00:52:18.800] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000749)  
We are committed to ensuring that every peso is translated into meaningful programs,

**[00:52:25.420] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000750)  
concrete services, and tangible benefits for our farmers and their families.

**[00:52:33.080] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000751)  
As we move forward, we remain guided by one enduring vision,

**[00:52:39.360] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000752)  
sariling lupa para sa bagong bukas na masagana.

**[00:52:44.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000753)  
Thank you for your patience. Maraming salamat po.

**[00:52:54.880] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000754)  
Maraming maraming salamat, Secretary Conrad Estrella,

**[00:52:59.400] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000755)  
for that detailed and comprehensive report on the agrarian reform.

**[00:53:06.360] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000756)  
May we now move on to interpolations and discussion.

**[00:53:11.480] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000757)  
May I remind our distinguished colleagues that each member is allotted five minutes for questions or interpolation,

**[00:53:19.740] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000758)  
excluding the responses of the resource persons.

**[00:53:23.060] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000759)  
When the bell sounds,

**[00:53:24.120] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000760)  
the member has 30 seconds to conclude.

**[00:53:27.640] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000761)  
Consistent with the long-standing practice of the House,

**[00:53:31.340] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000762)  
the Chair shall recognize minority, independent, and majority members in an alternating manner when practicable.

**[00:53:40.900] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000763)  
The Chair first recognizes the Honorable Representative Leila de Lima from the minority.

**[00:53:48.060] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000764)  
Ma'am?

**[00:53:49.340] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000765)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[00:53:52.020] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000766)  
Good afternoon.

**[00:53:52.780] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000767)  
Good afternoon.

**[00:53:52.980] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000768)  
Good afternoon.

**[00:53:54.120] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000769)  
Good afternoon to my colleagues and good afternoon to the DAR family, especially to the Secretary.

**[00:53:59.620] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000770)  
First, I wish to manifest for the record that I am fully supportive of the proposed budget for the DAR.

**[00:54:07.160] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000771)  
But I have, of course, issues to—I have questions to propound on certain issues.

**[00:54:13.200] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000772)  
I'm aware of the limited time.

**[00:54:14.740] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000773)  
I have only five minutes, so tingnan ko na lang po kung ilan ang makukover ng five minutes.

**[00:54:21.000] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000774)  
So, diretsuhan lang po tayo.

**[00:54:24.120] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000775)  
The split project.

**[00:54:26.360] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000776)  
Ano na po pinaka-status ng split?

**[00:54:28.300] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000777)  
Is it finished?

**[00:54:29.320] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000778)  
Is the World Bank loan exhausted?

**[00:54:32.440] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000779)  
Or did the department simply deprioritize the split?

**[00:54:37.040] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000780)  
The split is, of course, the support for parcelization for individual titling.

**[00:54:46.460] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000781)  
So, priority pa rin ho ba yung split project?

**[00:54:51.420] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000782)  
Is it still a DAR priority for 2027?

**[00:54:54.120] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000783)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[00:54:58.440] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000784)  
It is still a priority.

**[00:55:00.820] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000785)  
In fact, we are moving full speed ahead with the split program.

**[00:55:06.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000786)  
And we are proud to say, Your Honor, that no less than the World Bank, because this is a World Bank-funded project,

**[00:55:17.540] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000787)  
no less than the World Bank have announced that one of the—

**[00:55:24.120] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000788)  
best, or if not the best-performing agencies,

**[00:55:29.040] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000789)  
one of the best, or if not the best agencies that are implementing World Bank-funded projects

**[00:55:37.140] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000790)  
is the Department of Agrarian Reform.

**[00:55:39.740] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000791)  
And we really have to concentrate on this, Your Honor, especially this year,

**[00:55:46.900] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000792)  
because this is going to expire next year.

**[00:55:50.320] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000793)  
December 31st, 2027.

**[00:55:54.120] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000794)  
And we really need this, Your Honor, because this helps us in the parcelization of the collective cloas that were distributed in the past.

**[00:56:07.940] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000795)  
And this collective cloas, as we all know, did not really accelerate the distribution of titles.

**[00:56:16.300] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000796)  
Rather, it created the confusion and even conflict among the agrarian reform beneficiaries.

**[00:56:24.120] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000797)  
Because, Your Honor, well, in the collective cloas, there was no parcelization to speak of.

**[00:56:34.400] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000798)  
It was not specified in the title.

**[00:56:38.820] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000799)  
One title bearing numerous owners, but they don't know which part—

**[00:56:43.400] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000800)  
So, doon nagkakaroon ng problema?

**[00:56:45.060] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000801)  
Doon nagkakaroon ng problema.

**[00:56:47.580] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000802)  
And that is why the split came about so that we can already start—

**[00:56:54.120] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000803)  
the parcelization.

**[00:56:56.200] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000804)  
And we have been successful so far, Your Honor.

**[00:56:58.620] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000805)  
So, the World Bank loan has not been exhausted yet?

**[00:57:01.360] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000806)  
No, it has not been exhausted.

**[00:57:03.640] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000807)  
They are very strict with the approval of the releases, Your Honor.

**[00:57:13.320] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000808)  
So, gusto ko po sana makakuha ng data for 2025 and 2026.

**[00:57:18.380] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000809)  
How many collective cloas were validated, parcels were surveyed,

**[00:57:22.820] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000810)  
and e-titles were generated?

**[00:57:24.120] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000811)  
And actually distributed compared with DAR's annual targets?

**[00:57:30.880] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000812)  
Can DAR provide the figures per region?

**[00:57:33.520] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000813)  
You don't have to produce it now, but if you have it now, I'd be happy to have that.

**[00:57:40.320] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000814)  
How much of the split loan has also been disbursed to date?

**[00:57:45.380] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000815)  
Kasi nga yung limited time left for you to complete the program or the project.

**[00:57:50.920] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000816)  
Doon naman po tayo sa LTSB budget.

**[00:57:54.120] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000817)  
Based on 2027 NEP, may budget allocation po ang DAR ng 4.10 billion

**[00:58:00.160] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000818)  
para sa Land Tenure Security Program or yung LTSB.

**[00:58:05.500] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000819)  
Pero may libu-libong ARBs pa rin po na owners on paper.

**[00:58:09.720] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000820)  
Dahil hindi nila actual na napapasok, nahahawakan,

**[00:58:14.260] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000821)  
o na lilinang ang mga lupang iginawad sa kanila.

**[00:58:18.780] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000822)  
Magkano po sa 4.10 billion pesos allocation

**[00:58:22.800] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000823)  
ang direktang—

**[00:58:24.120] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000824)  
ilalaan para tugunan ng mga backlog

**[00:58:27.200] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000825)  
sa installation, land acquisition, compensation, at enforcement.

**[00:58:33.920] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000826)  
At ilang hectares po, at higit sa lahat,

**[00:58:36.020] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000827)  
ilang hectares at ilang ARBs po

**[00:58:39.200] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000828)  
ang konkretong commitment ng DAR

**[00:58:41.960] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000829)  
na mailalagay sa actual na posesyon,

**[00:58:46.160] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000830)  
sa posesyon at control ng mga beneficiaries

**[00:58:48.560] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000831)  
sa ilalim ng programang ito.

**[00:58:51.440] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000832)  
Your Honor,

**[00:58:52.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000833)  
if you will allow it,

**[00:58:55.340] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000834)  
our Undersecretary for FOO will answer that concern.

**[00:58:59.880] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000835)  
Yes, please.

**[00:59:01.000] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000835-split-2)  
Thank you.

**[00:59:01.540] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000836)  
May I request that from the Chair

**[00:59:05.660] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000837)  
that Undersecretary Keisel Celeste be recognized.

**[00:59:12.460] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000838)  
Thank you, Your Honor.

**[00:59:13.760] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000839)  
Ay, Yusek Keisel Celeste, you're recognized.

**[00:59:17.120] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000840)  
Thank you, Your Honor.

**[00:59:18.120] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000841)  
Mr. Chair, based on the budgetary ceiling provided,

**[00:59:22.280] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000842)  
for by the DBM,

**[00:59:23.880] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000843)  
tayo po ay binigyan lang po tayo

**[00:59:26.440] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000844)  
ng about 20,000 hectares to be funded each year.

**[00:59:32.320] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000845)  
So based on this 20,000 hectares funded each year,

**[00:59:35.460] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000846)  
nagbibigay po tayo ng about, let's say,

**[00:59:38.120] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000847)  
rough figure po, nasa 10,000 pesos per hectare

**[00:59:41.300] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000848)  
ang funded po natin for LTSP

**[00:59:45.360] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000849)  
from documentation down to distribution, Mr. Chair.

**[00:59:48.680] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000850)  
10,000 per hectare. Is that sufficient?

**[00:59:52.280] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000851)  
No, ma'am.

**[00:59:53.080] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000852)  
Absolutely.

**[00:59:53.960] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000853)  
No, ma'am.

**[00:59:54.920] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000854)  
Okay. Pero yung proposal nyo, was it the one granted now sa NEP?

**[01:00:00.000] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000855)  
Is that the same as your proposed amount?

**[01:00:02.580] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000856)  
No, ma'am. We could propose definitely a higher target,

**[01:00:07.200] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000857)  
but based on the budgetary ceiling provided for by the DVM,

**[01:00:10.140] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000858)  
we are constrained to have it budgeted up until only about 20,000 hectares per year.

**[01:00:16.420] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000859)  
And that would cover how many hectares again?

**[01:00:19.500] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000860)  
About 20,000 to 22,000. One is to one, ma'am.

**[01:00:22.880] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000861)  
Pwede na siguro yun for 2027?

**[01:00:26.180] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000862)  
Not enough, ma'am.

**[01:00:27.420] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000863)  
Not enough, definitely.

**[01:00:28.460] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000864)  
Doon naman po tayo sa debt condonation.

**[01:00:31.240] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000865)  
Nag-report po ang DAR na 57.6 billion of farmers' obligations ang nakondone.

**[01:00:37.260] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000866)  
Can you confirm it?

**[01:00:39.100] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000867)  
57.76 billion.

**[01:00:42.060] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000868)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[01:00:42.500] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000869)  
Kinabang umano ang mahigit 610,000 ARBs.

**[01:00:48.140] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000870)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[01:00:49.520] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000871)  
Ilang ARBs na po ang actual na nakatanggap ng debt condonation hanggang sa kasalukuyan?

**[01:00:54.460] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000872)  
Nandito.

**[01:00:55.620] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000873)  
At ilang porsyento po ito ng kabuang bilang ng ELP?

**[01:00:58.460] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000874)  
Eligible beneficiaries.

**[01:01:03.650] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000875)  
She would like to answer it, Your Honor.

**[01:01:06.250] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000876)  
Go ahead, please.

**[01:01:08.050] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000877)  
Your Honor, as of date, we have already distributed about 703,000 COCROMs to our ARBs.

**[01:01:16.050] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000878)  
It was also reported that may ilang mga ARBs po ang hindi qualified para sa debt condonation.

**[01:01:25.630] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000879)  
At sa halip ay isinailalim na lang ng moratorium.

**[01:01:30.830] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000880)  
Ilang ARBs po ang hindi naisama sa condonation program?

**[01:01:35.410] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000881)  
At ano-ano po yung mga dahilan kung bakit hindi sila kasama?

**[01:01:39.210] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000882)  
What are the reasons for their exclusion?

**[01:01:42.430] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000883)  
At magkano po ang kabuang halaga ng utang na nanatili nilang kailangang bayaran?

**[01:01:47.210] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000884)  
Your Honor, based on the data that we have right now,

**[01:01:50.250] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000885)  
the only COCROMs that we have not yet distributed are the ones that have been,

**[01:01:56.610] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000886)  
that are supposed to be distributed in the BARMM area.

**[01:01:59.510] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000887)  
But based on...

**[01:02:00.830] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000888)  
Your question po.

**[01:02:02.410] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000889)  
We will definitely validate this issue on where some of our ARBs have not allegedly been issued cockrobs.

**[01:02:12.250] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000890)  
The reasons for the exclusion.

**[01:02:14.950] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000891)  
I suppose you have ideas why they have been excluded.

**[01:02:19.010] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000892)  
Ano po ba yung mga usual?

**[01:02:27.060] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000893)  
One of the reasons why these ARBs may have been excluded, Your Honor,

**[01:02:32.440] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000894)  
is that the implementation of RA 11953.

**[01:02:36.840] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000895)  
has actually, has a cut-off of up until July 2020, 2024.

**[01:02:45.840] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000896)  
So yung mga na-disqualify, yung mga exclude, hindi na sila umabot?

**[01:02:49.180] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000897)  
So wala nang ibang rason?

**[01:02:50.660] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-000898)  
This could be a possible reason, ma'am, but we will definitely validate and provide your office with a more detailed explanation for this.

**[01:02:59.060] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000899)  
I would appreciate that.

**[01:03:00.380] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000900)  
Doon naman po tayo sa agricultural land conversion.

**[01:03:02.720] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000901)  
Patuloy pa rin po ang reports ng illegal agricultural land.

**[01:03:06.840] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000902)  
Agricultural land conversion despite existing regulations.

**[01:03:10.320] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000903)  
Magkano po ang inilaan ng DAR para sa monitoring, detection, at enforcement laban sa illegal conversions?

**[01:03:18.940] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000904)  
At ilan na po ang mga kasong na-aksyonan at successfully resolved ng DAR sa nakalipas na tatlong taon?

**[01:03:27.160] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000905)  
So these are illegal land conversion because there are indeed reports on this.

**[01:03:31.500] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000906)  
Your Honor.

**[01:03:33.040] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000907)  
Secretary.

**[01:03:33.560] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000908)  
Let me say that...

**[01:03:37.380] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000909)  
The illegal conversions, I don't think the DAR have done this, Your Honor.

**[01:03:43.880] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000910)  
But definitely there are many local government units that have made their own conversion, Your Honor.

**[01:03:53.720] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000911)  
And we are communicating with them also.

**[01:03:57.420] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000912)  
I suppose DAR has been monitoring those cases of illegal land conversion.

**[01:04:01.920] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000913)  
And you must have data and certain information about this.

**[01:04:05.440] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000914)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[01:04:05.700] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000915)  
And what do you do?

**[01:04:06.840] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000916)  
With regard to that, that you are...

**[01:04:08.040] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000917)  
We call their attention, Your Honor, that they have illegally converted these lands.

**[01:04:17.540] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000918)  
And then what happens next?

**[01:04:19.480] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000919)  
There are many, Your Honor, that they went back to DAR.

**[01:04:23.740] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000920)  
And they proceeded with the proper procedure, Your Honor.

**[01:04:31.280] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000921)  
In the first place, there's nothing to revoke because it is not...

**[01:04:35.480] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000922)  
Yes.

**[01:04:35.500] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000923)  
We don't recognize it.

**[01:04:36.840] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000924)  
Yes.

**[01:04:38.540] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000925)  
But we...

**[01:04:39.680] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000926)  
The others, we called their attention, Your Honor.

**[01:04:44.060] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000927)  
At least those that we have monitored.

**[01:04:46.940] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000928)  
And some of...

**[01:04:48.840] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000929)  
Many of them have complied so far, Your Honor.

**[01:04:51.880] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000930)  
Thank you for that.

**[01:04:52.600] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000931)  
How much time do I have?

**[01:04:54.900] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000932)  
Much time?

**[01:04:58.270] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000933)  
Hindi ko po nakikita yung timer.

**[01:05:00.070] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000934)  
You have 58 seconds, ma'am.

**[01:05:02.050] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000935)  
One minute.

**[01:05:03.050] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000936)  
One minute.

**[01:05:03.730] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000937)  
Okay.

**[01:05:03.970] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000938)  
So last...

**[01:05:05.970] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000939)  
Kasi one minute na lang.

**[01:05:07.590] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000940)  
I'd like to tackle...

**[01:05:08.570] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000941)  
The case of Sariaya, Quezon.

**[01:05:13.570] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000942)  
The Cloa Cancellation.

**[01:05:15.870] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000943)  
Buhay na kaso po ito, hindi ho ba?

**[01:05:19.070] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000944)  
Ang Cloa Cancellation at Land Conversion na nangyayari ngayon sa Sarayaya Quezon.

**[01:05:25.510] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000945)  
Based on reports.

**[01:05:27.290] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000946)  
May mga ulat po na ang mga magsasaka sa Manggalang Kiling,

**[01:05:31.230] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000947)  
Concepcion 1 at Concepcion Pinagbakuran

**[01:05:34.850] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000948)  
ay nawalan o posibleng mawalan ng kanilang mga Cloa.

**[01:05:38.570] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000949)  
So ang tanong ko po, ilang po, ilang Cloa po sa Sarayaya Quezon

**[01:05:43.350] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000950)  
ang na-revoke na o kasalukuyang subject to cancellation

**[01:05:47.150] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000951)  
at ilang ARBs po ang apektado?

**[01:05:52.410] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000952)  
Your Honor, with the permission of the Chairman and the members of the Appropriation Committee,

**[01:05:59.270] DAR Secretary Conrado M. Estrella III** (asr-000953)  
our Undersecretary for Legal Concerns would address the concern, Your Honor.

**[01:06:08.570] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000954)  
Yes, please.

**[01:06:09.370] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000955)  
Of course.

**[01:06:11.130] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000956)  
Yusek Napoleon Galit.

**[01:06:14.510] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-000957)  
Thank you.

**[01:06:16.250] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000958)  
Magandang umaga po,

**[01:06:18.890] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000959)  
Presiding Officers and Honorable Madam Layla.

**[01:06:25.150] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000960)  
Iyong pong tungkol sa Sarayaya Quezon,

**[01:06:27.510] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000961)  
there was a decision by the Supreme Court in 2018

**[01:06:30.790] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000962)  
where the DAR coverage was nullified

**[01:06:34.670] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000963)  
because the reason,

**[01:06:37.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000964)  
the subject property was already exempted

**[01:06:40.570] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000965)  
prior to the implementation of the CARP law

**[01:06:46.190] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000966)  
which was on June 15, 1988.

**[01:06:49.430] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000967)  
So the legal consequence of which is that

**[01:06:53.030] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000968)  
our ARBs are facing the consequences of having their Cloas being cancelled.

**[01:07:01.530] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000969)  
Now, the reason why this Cloas,

**[01:07:04.750] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000970)  
despite the decision of the Supreme Court,

**[01:07:06.650] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000971)  
are not yet cancelled,

**[01:07:07.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000972)  
most of them, because we are trying to mediate on the part

**[01:07:13.410] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000973)  
for and on behalf of the farmers, Madam Layla.

**[01:07:17.590] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000974)  
For an instance, we are fighting for them

**[01:07:20.710] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000975)  
under the rights under Republic Act 1199

**[01:07:25.730] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000976)  
as amended by 1163

**[01:07:29.690] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000977)  
and as further amended by Republic Act 3844.

**[01:07:33.690] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000978)  
They are entitled to the security of the land.

**[01:07:37.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000979)  
They are entitled to tenure and the disturbance compensation to their blissful being the tenant's right.

**[01:07:47.590] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000980)  
Now, we are likewise conducting further mediation proceedings

**[01:07:53.990] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000981)  
between the land owners who seems to have won the victory at the Supreme Court level.

**[01:08:00.530] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000982)  
And that is why these farmers in Sarayaya Quezon

**[01:08:03.930] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000983)  
are still occupying the land

**[01:08:06.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000984)  
and still farming the land.

**[01:08:07.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000985)  
And we continue to mediate despite the loss that the farmers have suffered.

**[01:08:14.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000986)  
And of course, including the DAR in 2018

**[01:08:18.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000987)  
on account of that decision by the Supreme Court.

**[01:08:21.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000988)  
Madam Layla.

**[01:08:22.830] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000989)  
Just a follow-up question on that.

**[01:08:26.830] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000990)  
So, hindi ba pinapakita po nito na may gaps or deficiencies sa naging proseso ng DAR

**[01:08:32.830] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000991)  
sa original land acquisition validation?

**[01:08:36.830] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000992)  
At titling?

**[01:08:38.830] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000993)  
Kung mayroon po, ano ang mga hakbang ng DAR

**[01:08:41.830] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000994)  
para maitama ang mga pagkukulang na ito at maiwasang maulit ang ganito mga kaso?

**[01:08:47.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000995)  
Madam, Madam Layla.

**[01:08:50.830] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-000996)  
There must be some deficiencies or gaps there in the original.

**[01:08:54.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000997)  
The decision, the situation which brought about this kind of situation

**[01:09:02.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000998)  
was because

**[01:09:04.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-000999)  
in the implementation of the CARP law, Madam Layla,

**[01:09:09.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-001000)  
the DAR normally is very desirous to cover every land holdings

**[01:09:17.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-001001)  
which has the character of a private agricultural or a government agricultural land holdings.

**[01:09:23.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-001002)  
Nonetheless, there are situations where after the coverage, the notice of coverage,

**[01:09:30.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-001003)  
and in fact, very sadly stated,

**[01:09:33.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-001004)  
even after CLUAS were issued,

**[01:09:35.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-001005)  
where a particular land owner came up with evidence for an instance

**[01:09:42.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-001006)  
of a reclassification issued by the local government units,

**[01:09:46.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-001007)  
which has an affirmative approval from the DSOD,

**[01:09:50.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-001008)  
recently DSOD before the HLURV or the NCV.

**[01:09:55.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-001009)  
And that is the reason why in the exercise of adjudicatory power of DAR,

**[01:10:01.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-001010)  
we adjudicate this case.

**[01:10:04.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-001011)  
However, despite the fact that several farmers have won at the level of the Office of the Secretary before the DAR,

**[01:10:14.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-001012)  
on appeal to the Court of Appeals and to the Supreme Court,

**[01:10:18.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-001013)  
we lost the case.

**[01:10:19.830] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-001014)  
This is the situation of the heirs of GALA versus the samahan ng mamamamayan ng saryaya.

**[01:10:29.530] Rep. Ian Paul L. Dy** (asr-001015)  
Your time is up, ma'am.

**[01:10:32.900] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001016)

**[01:10:33.900] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001017)

**[01:10:34.900] Rep. Leila M. de Lima** (asr-001018)  
Thank you.

Thank you.

**[01:13:58.300] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001056)

**[01:14:09.310] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001059)  
I think you are very much aware in the situation of si Roma.

**[01:14:13.310] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001060)

**[01:14:16.310] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001061)  
1,014.92 hectares property overlaps

**[01:14:20.310] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001062)  
with the holding of 537.53 hectares of alienable and disposable land.

**[01:14:28.310] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001063)  
438.5 hectares...

**[01:14:29.310] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001064)  
438.5 hectares of cloa.

**[01:14:33.310] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001065)  
And 341 hectares timberland.

**[01:14:37.310] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001066)

**[01:14:41.310] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001067)

**[01:14:46.310] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001068)

**[01:14:48.310] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001069)  
So, first, may question sa DAR.

**[01:14:52.310] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001070)

**[01:15:00.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001072)

**[01:15:45.630] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-001078)  
Sir, the total coverage of...

**[01:15:54.030] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001081)

**[01:15:57.480] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-001082)

**[01:15:58.360] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-001083)

**[01:16:05.520] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001084)

**[01:16:06.840] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-001085)

**[01:16:12.160] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-001086)

**[01:16:21.460] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-001087)  
for public.

**[01:16:22.280] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-001088)  
For partialization.

**[01:16:23.200] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001089)  
So, again, 1014, 745 ang may...

**[01:16:30.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001090)

**[01:16:31.580] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001091)  
Affected.

**[01:16:32.280] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001092)  
Wow.

**[01:16:33.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001093)  
RD, what's the definition of NARA settlement?

**[01:16:38.520] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-001094)

**[01:16:48.760] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001095)

**[01:16:51.460] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001096)

**[01:16:52.280] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-001097)  
Yes, po.

**[01:16:53.400] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-001098)  
Under Republica 6389, it was turned over to the DAR.

**[01:16:57.160] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001099)  
Well, the question is relevant because portions of the NARA settlement that are not yet covered by CLOA

**[01:17:02.580] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001100)  
have been specifically reserved distribution to the landless under said laws on agrarian reform.

**[01:17:11.160] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-001101)

**[01:17:18.960] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-001102)

**[01:17:19.740] DAR Undersecretary for Field Operations Atty. Kazel C. Celeste** (asr-001103)

Approximately 2,000.

R.O.D. of Camarines World.

Yes.

And on December 2025, DAR distributed a total of 3,497 land titles to 2,697 ARBs covering 5,929.36 hectares of agricultural land, including agricultural lands in Sonoma.

**[02:03:32.260] DAR Undersecretary for Legal Affairs Atty. Napoleon U. Galit** (asr-002375)
